/* Image:mask */

position: absolute;
left: 0px;
right: 0px;
top: 0px;
bottom: 0px;

background: linear-gradient(180deg, #000000 0%, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5) 100%);


/* Image */

position: absolute;
left: 0px;
right: 0px;
top: 0px;
bottom: 0px;

background: #FFFFFF;

position: absolute;

right: 0px;

right: 0px;

flex: none;

right: 0px;
top: 0px;

flex: none;
order: 2;
